"Other programs keep their sound. Windows mixes every program in steps of {0} ms on this device, so the buffer cannot go below that. For lower latency choose the exclusive mode or an ASIO driver." = "其他程序保留其声音。Windows在此设备上以{0} ms为步长混合每个程序，因此缓冲区不能低于该值。要获得更低延迟，请选择独占模式或ASIO驱动程序。"
"This device does not support a shorter period in shared mode (it stays at {0} ms). Use the exclusive mode or an ASIO driver for lower latency." = "此设备在共享模式下不支持更短的周期（保持在{0} ms）。要获得更低延迟，请使用独占模式或ASIO驱动程序。"
"Shared like the normal mode, but asks Windows for a shorter mixing period. Only some drivers support it." = "与普通模式一样共享，但向Windows请求更短的混合周期。仅部分驱动程序支持。"
"LYNX SHARE uses the device alone: the lowest latency without ASIO, but other programs go silent while LYNX SHARE plays." = "LYNX SHARE独占设备：不用ASIO时延迟最低，但LYNX SHARE播放时其他程序会无声。"

"LYNX ASIO (built in)" = "LYNX ASIO（内置）"
"LYNX SHARE's own ASIO driver, with any device: playback and recording (microphone) at low latency. \"Driver settings...\" chooses shared or exclusive mode, the output and input devices and shows the latency. For other programs, run \"Register LYNX ASIO\" in LYNX SHARE's folder once." = "LYNX SHARE自己的ASIO驱动程序，适用于任何设备：低延迟播放和录音（麦克风）。“驱动程序设置...”可选择共享或独占模式、输出和输入设备，并显示延迟。如需供其他程序使用，请在LYNX SHARE文件夹中运行一次“Register LYNX ASIO”。"
"LYNX SHARE's own ASIO driver, with any device: playback and recording (microphone) at low latency. \"Driver settings...\" chooses shared or exclusive mode, the output and input devices and shows the latency. To use it in other programs, install LYNX SHARE from lynx-share.com (the installer registers it)." = "LYNX SHARE自带的ASIO驱动程序，适用于任何设备：低延迟播放和录音（麦克风）。“驱动程序设置...”可选择共享或独占模式、输出和输入设备，并显示延迟。若要在其他程序中使用，请从lynx-share.com安装LYNX SHARE（安装程序会注册该驱动）。"

# --- Round 145: Ridge Strip's Vocal strip voicing
"Compander" = "压扩器"
"Make-up" = "补偿增益"
"Soft knee" = "软拐点"
"Broadband|Split" = "宽带|分频"
"Look" = "外观"
"De-ploser" = "爆破音消除器"
"Equaliser" = "均衡器"
"Vocal strip voicing" = "人声通道条音色风格"
"The vocal channel strip: de-esser, de-ploser, three-band EQ and compander in any order" = "人声通道条：齿音消除器、爆破音消除器、三段EQ和压扩器，顺序任意"
"The order of the four vocal modules" = "四个人声模块的顺序"
"Turns down sibilance: the highs when they get loud compared with the rest of the voice" = "压低齿音：当高频相对于人声其余部分变响时将其压低"
"How strong the highs must be, compared with the whole voice, before the de-esser works" = "高频相对于整个人声需要多强，齿音消除器才开始工作"
"The frequency above which the de-esser listens" = "齿音消除器侦听的频率下限"
"Split: only the highs are turned down; broadband: the whole voice" = "分频：只压低高频；宽带：压低整个人声"
"Delays the audio 1 ms so the de-esser acts before the sibilant starts" = "将音频延时1 ms，使齿音消除器在齿音开始前起作用"
"Plays only the band the de-esser acts on" = "只播放齿音消除器作用的频段"
"Turns down plosives: the lows when they get loud compared with the rest of the voice" = "压低爆破音：当低频相对于人声其余部分变响时将其压低"
"How strong the lows must be, compared with the whole voice, before the de-ploser works" = "低频相对于整个人声需要多强，爆破音消除器才开始工作"
"The frequency below which the de-ploser listens" = "爆破音消除器侦听的频率上限"
"Split: only the lows are turned down; broadband: the whole voice" = "分频：只压低低频；宽带：压低整个人声"
"Delays the audio 10 ms so the de-ploser acts before the pop starts" = "将音频延时10 ms，使爆破音消除器在爆破音开始前起作用"
"Plays only the band the de-ploser acts on" = "只播放爆破音消除器作用的频段"
"Switches the vocal EQ on" = "开启人声EQ"
"High-pass filter with a small lift above its frequency" = "高通滤波器，在其频率以上略有提升"
"A narrow cut (or boost) for one resonance" = "针对单个共振的窄切除（或提升）"
"A wide boost or cut for presence and air" = "针对临场感和空气感的宽提升或衰减"
"Expander, then compressor, make-up and drive" = "扩展器，然后是压缩器、补偿增益和驱动"
"Below this level the expander turns the signal down (fully left = off)" = "低于此电平时扩展器压低信号（最左 = 关）"
"Gain after the compressor" = "压缩器之后的增益"
"A gradual start of the compression around the threshold" = "在阈值附近逐渐开始压缩"
"Soft clipping after the make-up gain: warmer, louder; stronger with more make-up" = "补偿增益之后的软削波：更温暖、更响；补偿越多越强"
"Expander" = "扩展器"
"Add to key" = "添加到调"
"Add to key: the notes are played the root note's distance from C5 away (instruments that are not samplers)" = "添加到调：音符按根音与C5的距离移调演奏（非采样器的乐器）"
"Arpeggiator repeat: times the arpeggio plays in an octave before the next one (0: the chord at once)" = "琶音器重复：琶音在一个八度内播放的次数，然后进入下一个八度（0：和弦一次性播放）"
"Category" = "类别"
"Chord" = "和弦"
"Fat mode: a second, mirrored series of echoes (opposite pan and pitch steps), all at the feedback level" = "Fat模式：第二组镜像回声（声像和音高步进相反），均为反馈电平"
"Latency in beats" = "延迟（拍）"

# --- round 155: export plugin warm-up
"Plugin warm-up" = "插件预热"
"Before the export starts, third-party plugins process a moment of silence until they have finished loading their settings (bridged plugins, samplers that load in the background), so the file does not start with half-loaded effects" = "导出开始前，第三方插件会先处理一段静音，直到加载完设置（桥接插件、在后台加载的采样器），以免文件开头出现未加载完的效果"

# --- Round 163c: sample pitch (audio clips follow the pitch, keep length, Pitch...)
"The sample's pitch in semitones (0.5 = 50 cents). The Piano Roll's keys play it chromatically around its root note (Misc › Root note, C5 by default); right-click the channel › Pitch... for semitones + cents" = "采样的音高，以半音为单位（0.5 = 50音分）。钢琴卷帘的琴键围绕其根音半音阶地演奏它（杂项 › 根音，默认为C5）；右键单击通道 › 音高... 可设置半音 + 音分"
"Keep length (stretch engine)" = "保持长度（拉伸）"
"On: the pitch, the fine tune and the keys change the pitch without changing the length, and \"Stretch\" changes the length without changing the pitch (like FL's Stretch modes). Off: resample, pitch and speed change together" = "开：音高、微调和琴键改变音高而不改变长度，“拉伸”改变长度而不改变音高（类似FL的Stretch模式）。关：重采样，音高和速度一起改变"
"The audio channel's pitch in semitones (0.5 = 50 cents): every clip of this file follows it. With \"Keep length\" the length stays, else the speed changes like a tape" = "音频通道的音高，以半音为单位（0.5 = 50音分）：此文件的每个片段都会跟随它。开启“保持长度”时长度不变，否则速度像磁带一样改变"
"On: the stretch and the pitch change separately (the stretch keeps the pitch, the pitch keeps the length). Off: resample, pitch and speed change together" = "开：拉伸和音高分别改变（拉伸保持音高，音高保持长度）。关：重采样，音高和速度一起改变"
"Pitch... (semitones, cents, keep length)" = "音高...（半音、音分、保持长度）"
"Transposes this audio clip's file (every clip of it follows)." = "对此音频片段的文件移调（其所有片段都会跟随）。"
"Transposes the sample. The Piano Roll's keys still play it chromatically around its root note." = "对采样移调。钢琴卷帘的琴键仍会围绕其根音半音阶地演奏它。"
"Semitones (-24 to +24)" = "半音（-24到+24）"
"Cents (-100 to +100)" = "音分（-100到+100）"
"Off: resample, like a tape: higher is shorter, lower is longer." = "关：重采样，像磁带一样：越高越短，越低越长。"
"Clip menu: [LMB] or [RMB] opens it (pitch, fit to tempo, reverse, chop, make unique...)" = "片段菜单：[LMB] 或 [RMB] 打开（音高、适配速度、倒放、切碎、设为唯一...）"
"(pitch: [RMB] › Pitch...)" = "（音高：[RMB] › 音高...）"
